| **amount** | `-50` | **Negative value** - If this represents a payment or currency amount, negative values are typically not allowed (unless specifically for refunds/credits) |

## Summary

- **Critical**: Invalid email, date, and phone format
- **Business logic**: Age out of range, potentially negative amount

I can offer to look up the invoice details or send an email if needed, but I cannot perform the cancellation/refund.
